Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The population variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 405 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 451 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the population decomposition. By minimality, the element a^405 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 451-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
